This completes the proof.
∎

Step 2
: Bounding the Adversarial Term
\Delta_{t,2}
.
Before discussing the bound on the adversarial noise term
\Delta_{t,2}
under the asynchronous sampling model, we first fix some notations that will be used frequently in Lemmas
6
and
7
. Denote by
\mathcal{N}_{t}(s,a)
a random variable which represents the count of the number of times the state-action pair
(s,a)
has been visited up to (and including) time
t
. Here,
\mathbbm{1}_{k}(s,a)
denotes the indicator variable that takes the value 1 if the state-action pair
(s_{k},a_{k})
at iteration
k
is equal to
(s,a)
, and 0 otherwise.
Thus, we observe the fact that
\mathcal{N}_{t}(s,a)=\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{k\in[t]}\mathbbm{1}_{k}(s,a)
. Under the i.i.d. sampling model, the probability of visiting a particular
(s,a)
pair at each time-step is given by
\lambda(s,a)=\pi(s)\mu(a|s).
As a result, the following is true:

\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{N}_{t}(s,a)\right]=\lambda(s,a)t.

(45)

Building on the above fact, we now construct a “good event" of sufficient measure on which, after a burn-in time, the number of visits to each state-action pair will concentrate around its mean value. To that end, we have the following simple application of Bernstein’s inequality.

Lemma 6
.

(
Constructing Good Event
) There exists an event
\mathcal{K}
of measure at least
1-\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta_1}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta_1}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta_1}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta_1}{4}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta_1}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta_1}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta_1}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta_1}{4}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta_1}{4}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta_1}{4}}}
, on which, the following holds simultaneously
\forall(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
,
\forall t\geq\bar{T}
:

\mathcal{N}_{t}(s,a)\geq\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}}\lambda_{\texttt{min}}\cdot t,

where
\bar{T}=\Big\lceil\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{104}{3\lambda_{\texttt{min}}}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{104}{3\lambda_{\texttt{min}}}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{104}{3\lambda_{\texttt{min}}}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{104}{3\lambda_{\texttt{min}}}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{104}{3\lambda_{\texttt{min}}}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{104}{3\lambda_{\texttt{min}}}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{104}{3\lambda_{\texttt{min}}}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{104}{3\lambda_{\texttt{min}}}}$}}{\raise |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{8 |\mc{S}||\mc{A}|T}{\delta_1}}}\right)\Big\rceil
.

Proof.

We start by writing
\mathcal{N}_{t}(s,a)=\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{k\in[t]}\mathbbm{1}_{k}(s,a)
, and observing the following basic facts:
\mathbb{E}[\mathbbm{1}_{k}(s,a)]=\lambda(s,a)
, and
\texttt{Var}[\mathbbm{1}_{k}(s,a))]\leq\lambda(s,a)
. For a fixed
(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
and fixed
t\in T
, the probability of the following event
\mathcal{K}_{1}^{\texttt{C}}(s,a,t)=\{\mathcal{N}_{t}(s,a)\leq\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}}\lambda(s,a)t\}
can be bounded using
Bernstein’s inequality:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{K}_{1}^{\texttt{C}}(s,a,t))

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\left(\Big\{\mathcal{N}_{t}(s,a)\leq\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}}\lambda(s,a)t\Big\}\right)

(46)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}\left(\Big\{\Big\lvert\mathcal{N}_{t}(s,a)-\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{N}_{t}(s,a)\right]\Big\rvert\geq\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise Let us set

. Thus, for a fixed state-action pair

, and a fixed

:

Lemma 7
.

(
Bounding Adversarial Corruption in
Robust Async-Q
) With probability at least
1-\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta}{2}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta}{2}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta}{2}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta}{2}}}
, the following bound holds simultaneously
\forall t\in[T]
:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise .

(50)

Next, let us borrow the good event
\mathcal{K}
from Lemma
6
, and decompose the complement of the event
\mathcal{E}_{k}
described in Eq. (
49
) as follows:

\{\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\texttt{C}}\}:=\{\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\texttt{C}}\}\cap\{\mathcal{K}\cup\mathcal{K}^{\texttt{C}}\}=\{\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\texttt{C}}\cap\mathcal{K}\}\cup\{\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\texttt{C}}\cap\mathcal{K}^{\texttt{C}}\}.

(51)

This immediately implies the following:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\texttt{C}})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\texttt{C}}\cap\mathcal{K})+\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\texttt{C}}\cap\mathcal{K}^{\texttt{C}}),

(52)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\texttt{C}}\cap\mathcal{K})+\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{K}^{\texttt{C}}).

From Lemma
6
, on the good event
\mathcal{K}
, we know that for
t\geq\bar{T}
, the following holds:
\mathcal{N}_{t}(s,a)\geq\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}}\lambda_{\texttt{min}}t
for all state-action pairs
(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
. Next, we establish a bound on
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\texttt{C}}\cap\mathcal{K})
in Eq. (
52
) as follows:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\texttt{C}}\cap\mathcal{K})

\displaystyle=\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{j=\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}}\lambda_{\texttt{min}}k}^{k}\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\texttt{C}}\cap\mathcal{K}\cap\{\mathcal{N}_{k}(s_{k},a_{k})=j\}\right),

(53)

\displaystyle\leq\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{j=\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}}\lambda_{\texttt{min}}k}^{k}\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\texttt{C}}\cap\{\mathcal{N}_{k}(s_{k},a_{k})=j\}\right),

\displaystyle\leq\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{j=\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}}\lambda_{\texttt{min}}k}^{k}\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\texttt{C}}\lvert\{\mathcal{N}_{k}(s_{k},a_{k})=j\}\right)\cdot\mathbb{P}\left(\{\mathcal{N}_{k}(s_{k},a_{k})=j\}\right),

\displaystyle\overset{(\bullet)}{\leq}\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{j=\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}}\lambda_{\texttt{min}}k}^{k}\mathbb{P}\left(\mathcal{E}_{k,1}^{\texttt{C}}\lvert\{\mathcal{N}_{k}(s_{k},a_{k})=j\}\right)\cdot\mathbb{P}\left(\{\mathcal{N}_{k}(s_{k},a_{k})=j\}\right),

\displaystyle\overset{(\bullet\bullet)}{\leq}\delta_{1}\cdot\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{j=\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{3}{4}}}\lambda_{\texttt{min}}k}^{k}\mathbb{P}\left(\{\mathcal{N}_{k}(s_{k},a_{k})=j\}\right),

In
(\bullet)
, for any fixed

, the deviation bound specified by the event

, we can leverage the robust mean guarantee in Theorem

), we can further simplify our decomposition in Eq. (

) as follows:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}_{k}^{\texttt{C}})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{E}^{\texttt{C}}\cap\mathcal{K})+\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{K}^{\texttt{C}}),

\displaystyle\overset{(*)}{\leq}\delta_{1}+\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta_1}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta_1}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta_1}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta_1}{4}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta_1}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta_1}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta_1}{4}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta_1}{4}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta_1}{4}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\delta_1}{4}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\delta_1}{4}}}\leq 2\delta_{1}.

In step
(*)
, we applied the upper bound on the probability of the good event
\mathcal{K}
established in Lemma
6
. Combining these results, we conclude that the following holds for a fixed

In
(\dagger)
, we used the bounds obtained for
Case I
and
Case II
. In
(\dagger\dagger)
, we simply used the monotonicity of logarithms and substituted
\delta_{1}=\delta/4T
. Lastly, combining our separate analyses for
Case I
and
Case II
leads to the claim of the lemma.
∎

Finite-Time Rates for
Robust Async-Q
(Proof of Theorem
2
)
: Having established Lemmas
4
,
5
,
6
, and
7
, we are now ready to proceed with the proof of the bound stated in Theorem
2
. First, to build intuition for the nature of the final bound, let us consider Eq. (
31
) in the absence of any contributions from noise or adversaries. In this case, the recursion simplifies to the idealized update rule:
Q_{t+1}=(I-\alpha D)Q_{t}+\alpha D(\mathcal{T}Q_{t})
. Subtracting the fixed point
Q^{*}
, which satisfies
Q^{*}=\mathcal{T}Q^{*}
, we obtain the error recursion
Q_{t+1}-Q^{*}=(I-\alpha D)(Q_{t}-Q^{*})+\alpha D(\mathcal{T}Q_{t}-\mathcal{T}Q^{*})
. Defining
d_{t}(s,a):=|Q_{t}(s,a)-Q^{*}(s,a)|
, and applying the contractiveness of the Bellman optimality operator under the
\infty
-norm, we can then obtain the following for each state-action pair
(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
:

\displaystyle d_{t+1}(s,a)

\displaystyle\leq(1-\alpha\lambda(s,a))d_{t}(s,a)+\alpha\gamma\lambda(s,a)\|d_{t}\|_{\infty},

(64)

\displaystyle\leq\left(1-\alpha\lambda_{\min}(1-\gamma)\right)\|d_{t}\|_{\infty}

Since this upper bound holds uniformly over all
(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
, we conclude:

\|d_{t+1}\|_{\infty}\leq\left(1-\alpha\lambda_{\min}(1-\gamma)\right)\|d_{t}\|_{\infty}.

(65)

Unrolling this recursion yields the following for all
t\in[T]
:

\|d_{t}\|_{\infty}\leq\left(1-\alpha\lambda_{\min}(1-\gamma)\right)^{t}\|d_{0}\|_{\infty}.

(66)

The goal is to now establish a similar recursion for our setting, while accounting for noise and adversarial corruption. To do so, we note that based on Lemma
5
and Lemma
7
, there exists an event - say
\mathcal{Y}
- of measure at least
1-\delta
, on which,
\rVert\Delta_{t,1}\lVert_{\infty}+\lVert\Delta_{t,2}\rVert_{\infty}\leq\bar{\Delta}_{t,1}+\bar{\Delta}_{t,2}\triangleq\Delta,\forall t\in[T]
, where
\Delta_{t,1}
and
\Delta_{t,2}

For

t+1

) and analyze it component-wise. In order to simplify the notation for algebraic decompositions in the subsequent steps, for two given functions
\{Q_{1},Q_{2}\}
and their corresponding mappings
\{\mathcal{T}Q_{1},\mathcal{T}Q_{2}\}
under the influence of the Bellman operator, we denote their component-wise difference as:

\displaystyle[Q_{1}-Q_{2}](s,a)\triangleq Q_{1}(s,a)-Q_{2}(s,a)

(68)

\displaystyle[\mathcal{T}Q_{1}-\mathcal{T}Q_{2}](s,a)\triangleq\mathcal{T}Q_{1}(s,a)-\mathcal{T}Q_{2}(s,a).

Similarly, we denote the
(s,a)
-th component of
\Delta_{t}
defined in Eq. (

), as
\Delta_{t}(s,a)
. Now, we proceed component wise, where the

\displaystyle+\alpha\lambda(s,a)\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{k=0}^{t}(1-\alpha\lambda(s,a))^{t-k}[\mathcal{T}Q_{k}-\mathcal{T}Q^{*}](s,a)+\Delta_{t}(s,a).

Appendix F
Proof of
Fundamental Lower Bound
in Theorem
3

In this section, we prove the lower bound stated in Theorem
3
. The proof is based on constructing two carefully designed observation models under a simple synchronous Huber contamination setting outlined in
[
59
,
60
,
61
]
, where at each round the learner receives corrupted or clean rewards for all state-action pairs simultaneously.
We begin by outlining the core intuition before delving into the technical details. We carefully construct two MDPs that satisfy two crucial properties: (i) the optimal state-action value functions corresponding to the constructed MDPs differ by
\Omega(\sqrt{\varepsilon})
, and (ii) under the Huber contamination model, the observed reward distributions are identical across the two MDPs. This setup ensures that no estimator can reliably distinguish between the two MDPs based on the contaminated observations alone, thereby forcing any estimator to incur an error of at least
\Omega(\sqrt{\varepsilon})
in the worst case. We now proceed to construct this adversarial instance and formalize the argument.

Step 1

(MDP Construction).
To construct the lower bound instance, we consider two MDPs that have a single common state
s
and a single common action
a
, such that the only source of randomness arises from the observed reward for the state-action pair
(s,a)
. Slightly departing from the notation introduced earlier in the prelude to Theorem
3
, we use indices
i=1
and
i=2
to represent objects associated with MDP 1 and MDP 2, respectively. The true noisy reward distributions
\mathcal{R}_{1}(s,a)
and
\mathcal{R}_{2}(s,a)
associated with MDPs 1 and 2 are as follows:

\varepsilon)}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\bar{\sigma}^2}{4(1 - \varepsilon)}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\bar{\sigma}^2}{4(1 - \varepsilon)}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\bar{\sigma}^2}{4(1 - \varepsilon)}}}<0.5\bar{\sigma}^{2},

(78)

where we have used the assumption that
\varepsilon<0.5
. Thus, each reward model has a finite variance uniformly bounded above by
\bar{\sigma}^{2}
. Since there is only one state-action pair, the optimal
Q
-value in each MDP is given by:

Q_{i}^{*}(s,a)=\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{R_i}{1 - \gamma}}},\quad i\in\{1,2\}.

(79)

Step 2

(Construction of Corrupted Observation Models.)
We now construct adversarial reward contaminations following the Huber contamination model. For each MDP
i\in\{1,2\}
, the observed reward at the state-action pair
(s,a)
is drawn with probability
1-\varepsilon
from the true underlying distribution
\mathcal{R}_{i}(s,a)
, and with probability
\varepsilon
from the adversarial distribution
\mathcal{Q}_{i}
. The distributions
\mathcal{Q}_{i}
,
i\in\{1,2\}
, represent the corruption distributions, as defined below in Eq. (
80
) and Eq. (
81
). Now subject to corruption based on these adversarial distributions, let the resulting reward distributions for MDPs 1 and 2 be denoted by
\tilde{R}_{1}
and
\tilde{R}_{2}
, respectively, where
\tilde{R}_{i}=(1-\varepsilon)\mathcal{R}_{i}(s,a)+\varepsilon\mathcal{Q}_{i},i=1,2
. These resulting distributions can be easily computed, and are shown in Eq. (
80
) and Eq. (
81
).

Step 3

(Lower Bound on Estimation Error.)
For
i=1,\ldots,T
, let
(X_{i},Y_{i})
be independent pairs of random observations satisfying:

\mathbb{P}(X_{i}=Y_{i}=-\bar{\sigma}/{\sqrt{\varepsilon}})=\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\varepsilon}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\varepsilon}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\varepsilon}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\varepsilon}{2}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\varepsilon}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\varepsilon}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\varepsilon}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\varepsilon}{2}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\varepsilon}{2}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\varepsilon}{2}}},

\,\mathbb{P}(X_{i}=Y_{i}=0)=1-\varepsilon\,,\,\mathbb{P}(X_{i}=Y_{i}=\bar{\sigma}/{\sqrt{\varepsilon}})=\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\varepsilon}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\varepsilon}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\varepsilon}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\varepsilon}{2}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\varepsilon}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\varepsilon}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\varepsilon}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\varepsilon}{2}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\varepsilon}{2}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{\varepsilon}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\varepsilon}{2}}}.

Let us note that
X_{i}
is distributed as per
\tilde{R}_{1}
, and
Y_{i}
as per
\tilde{R}_{2}
. Clearly, the following is true:
\mathbb{P}\left(\{X_{i}\}_{i\in[T]}=\{Y_{i}\}_{i\in[T]}\right)=1.
Now, suppose
\hat{R}_{T}
and
\hat{Q}_{T}
are estimators for the mean rewards and optimal state-action value functions, respectively, in the two MDPs. As we shall see, establishing a fundamental limit on the performance of
\hat{R}_{T}
is sufficient to establish a limit on the performance of
\hat{Q}_{T}
. To see this, start by noting that

\displaystyle

The finite-time performance of

is established in Theorem

\Delta_{t,2}
. We begin by analyzing the contribution of the adversarial corruption term, before turning to the non-adversarial noisy component. The latter necessitates a more refined and intricate analysis, as will become evident in the sequel.

. Since the threshold function defined in Eq. (
9
) is agnostic to the underlying reward statistics, we introduce an auxiliary time-step
\tilde{T}:=\max\left\{\tilde{\sigma}^{1/p},\,\bar{T}\right\}
, where
\bar{T}
was previously defined in Eq. (
5
), and recall that
p
is the parameter in the function
m(t)=t^{p}
that appears in the modified threshold (
9
).

Case I:
Consider first the case where
t\leq\tilde{T}.
We further split up this case into two sub-cases: one where
\tilde{T}=\bar{T}
, and the other where
\tilde{T}=\tilde{\sigma}^{1/p}
. We separately analyze these sub-cases below.

•

Suppose
\tilde{T}=\bar{T}
, which implies
t\leq\bar{T}
. Then, by the definition of the threshold function in Eq. (
9
), we have
\tilde{r}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})=0
. Consequently, just like in Case 1 of Lemma
7
, in this case we have
\|\zeta_{t,2}\|_{\infty}\leq\tilde{\sigma}.

•

Next, when
\tilde{T}=\tilde{\sigma}^{1/p}
, and
t\in[\bar{T},\tilde{T}]
, we can use the reward-agnostic threshold function defined in Eq. (
9
) to bound
\|\zeta_{t,2}\|_{\infty}
. To see how, start by noting that the following is always true deterministically:
|\tilde{r}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})|\leq\tilde{G}_{t},\forall t\geq 0.
Using
m(t)=t^{p}
in Eq. (
9
), and the fact that
t\geq\bar{T}
, we note that for
t\in[\bar{T},\tilde{T}]
, the following is true:
\tilde{G}_{t}\leq 3\mathcal{C}t^{p}\leq 3\mathcal{C}\tilde{T}^{p}=3\mathcal{C}\tilde{\sigma}
, where in the last step, we used that in this case
\tilde{T}=\tilde{\sigma}^{1/p}
. Thus, for
t\in[\bar{T},\tilde{T}]
, we have
|\tilde{r}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})|\leq 3\mathcal{C}\tilde{\sigma}
. As a result, we have
\|\zeta_{t,2}\|_{\infty}=|\tilde{r}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-R(s_{t},a_{t})|\leq 3\mathcal{C}\tilde{\sigma}+\bar{R}\leq 4\mathcal{C}\tilde{\sigma}
, since
\mathcal{C}\geq 1
, and
\bar{R}\leq\tilde{\sigma}
.

From our analysis of the two sub-cases above, we conclude that for
t\leq\tilde{T}
,
\|\zeta_{t,2}\|_{\infty}\leq 4\mathcal{C}\tilde{\sigma}
.
Next, we bound the adversarial corruption term
\Delta_{t,2}
in the
\infty
-norm for all
t\in[\tilde{T}]
as follows:

\displaystyle\|\Delta_{t,2}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\alpha\left\|\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{k=0}^{t}(I-\alpha D)^{t-k}\zeta_{k,2}\right\|_{\infty}

(88)

\displaystyle\overset{(*)}{\leq}\alpha\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{k=0}^{\tilde{T}}\|(I-\alpha D)^{t-k}\|_{\infty}\cdot\|\zeta_{k,2}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\overset{(**)}{\leq}8\mathcal{C}\alpha\tilde{\sigma}\tilde{T}.

In
(*)
, we first apply the triangle inequality, then use the sub-multiplicative property of the
\infty
-norm, and without loss of generality, assume
\tilde{T}\geq 1
. In
(**)
, we use the fact that
\|(I-\alpha D)^{t-k}\|_{\infty}\leq 1
, and that
\|\zeta_{k,2}\|_{\infty}\leq 4\mathcal{C}\tilde{\sigma}
, as established earlier for Case I. This completes the analysis for Case I.

Case II:
We now consider the case when
t>\tilde{T}
. Since
\tilde{T}:=\max\left\{\tilde{\sigma}^{1/p},\,\bar{T}\right\}
, it follows that
t>\tilde{T}\Rightarrow t>\bar{T}
. Now recall from the analysis of Lemma
7
that there exists an event
\mathcal{J}
of measure at least
1-2\delta_{1}T\geq 1-\delta/2
, on which, the following holds
simultaneously for all time steps
t\in[\bar{T}+1,T]
:

\left|\bar{r}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-R(s_{t},a_{t})\right|\leq\mathcal{C}\tilde{\sigma}\left(\sqrt{\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{4}{3}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{4}{3}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{4}{3}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{4}{3}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{4}{3}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{4}{3}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{4}{3}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{4}{3}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{4}{3}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{4}{3}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{4}{3}}}\cdot\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise }}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{ \log\left( \frac{4}{\delta_1} \right) }{ \lambda_{\texttt{min}} t }}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{ \log\left( \frac{4}{\delta_1} \right) (89)

On this event, we further have that for
t>\bar{T}
:
|\bar{r}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})|\leq G_{t}
, where
G_{t}
is the original threshold defined in (
6
). While this condition was enough to prevent any thresholding on event
\mathcal{J}
for
t>\bar{T}
for
Robust Async-Q
, it does not immediately imply that thresholding will not take place for
Robust Async-RAQ
. The reason for this stems from the fact that in the new algorithm, the modified threshold
\tilde{G}_{t}
in (
9
) can be an under-approximation of
G_{t}
during the period
[\bar{T},\tilde{T}]
. However, for
t>\tilde{T}
, we have
m(t)=t^{p}>\tilde{T}^{p}\geq\tilde{\sigma}
, since
\tilde{T}=\max\{\tilde{\sigma}^{1/p},\bar{T}\}.
As a result, for
t>\tilde{T}
, we have
G_{t}\leq\tilde{G}_{t}.
Consequently, on the event
\mathcal{J}
, we have that for all
t>\tilde{T}
,
|\bar{r}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})|\leq G_{t}<\tilde{G}_{t}
. Thus, the thresholding operation in line 7 will get bypassed, ensuring that
\tilde{r}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})=\bar{r}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})
, and, as a result, we conclude based on (
89
) that on event
\mathcal{J}
, for all
t>\tilde{T}
, the following is true:

\left|\tilde{r}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})-R(s_{t},a_{t})\right|\leq\mathcal{C}\tilde{\sigma}\left(\sqrt{\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise as follows:

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{k=0}^{t}\alpha(I-\alpha D)^{t-k}\mathcal{\zeta}_{k,2}\right\rVert_{\infty}\leq\left\lVert\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{k=0}^{\tilde{T}}\alpha(I-\alpha D)^{t-k}\mathcal{\zeta}_{k,2}\right\rVert_{\infty}+\left\lVert\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{k=\tilde{T}+1}^{t}\alpha(I-\alpha D)^{t-k}\mathcal{\zeta}_{k,2}\right\rVert_{\infty},

\lambda_{\min} }}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{\mc{C} \tilde{\sigma} \sqrt{\varepsilon} }{ \lambda_{\min} }}}\right)\triangleq\tilde{\Delta}_{t,2}.

For the first step, we stitched together the bounds for Cases I and II, and followed a similar reasoning as in the proof of Lemma
7
. Under the assumption
T\geq\tilde{T}
, we further used
{\tilde{T}}\leq\sqrt{\tilde{T}}\cdot\sqrt{{T}}
. Finally, we leveraged the definition
\tilde{T}=\max\left\{\bar{T},\,\tilde{\sigma}^{1/p}\right\}
, which implies
\tilde{T}\leq\bar{T}+\tilde{\sigma}^{1/p}
, and plugged in the expression for
\bar{T}
from (
5
), followed by simplifications. Combining the bounds obtained in
Case I
and
Case II
, we conclude the proof of Lemma
8
.
∎

Step 2
: Bound the Non-Adversarial Noise Term
\Delta_{t,1}
.
We now proceed to the more delicate part of the analysis that involves controlling the effect of noise. Like before, to control the noise effect using a martingale-based argument, we will derive uniform bounds on the iterates generated by
Robust Async-RAQ
. However, as a departure from the analysis in Appendix
E
, we will derive two sets of bounds: crude bounds that hold deterministically, and finer bounds that hold with high probability. The rationale for this will become clearer soon. We start with the cruder bounds.

Proof.

The proof is nearly identical to that of Lemma

, with the only difference arising from the modified threshold function. Let us start by noting that the following is always true deterministically:
|\tilde{r}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})|\leq\tilde{G}_{t},\forall t\geq 0.
Now based on the definition of the modified threshold
\tilde{G}_{t}
in (
9

\bar{T}
in (
5

\tilde{G}_{t}=0,\forall t\leq\bar{T}

\tilde{G}_{t}\leq 3\mathcal{C}t^{p}\leq 3\mathcal{C}T^{p},\forall t>\bar{T}.
As a result, in
Robust Async-RAQ

\tilde{r}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})

|\tilde{r}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})|\leq\tilde{G}_{t}\leq 3\mathcal{C}T^{p},\forall t\in[T].

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3 \mathcal{C} T^p}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{3 \mathcal{C} T^p}{1 - \gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{3 \mathcal{C} T^p}{1 - For (a), we used
|R(s,a)|\leq\tilde{\sigma}
and Eq. (
93
). For (b), we used the fact that
T\geq\tilde{T}\implies T^{p}\geq(\tilde{T})^{p}\geq\tilde{\sigma}\geq|R(s,a)|
. As a result,
|R(s,a)|\leq 3\mathcal{C}T^{p}.
Since our analysis above holds for
any
state-action pair, we conclude that
\|\mathcal{T}Q_{t}\|_{\infty}\leq 3\mathcal{C}T^{p}/(1-\gamma).

\zeta_{t,1}
as follows:

where (a) follows from (
94
) and (b) from (

) and the bound we derived on

. This concludes the proof.
∎

At this stage, it is instructive to compare the bound on
\|\zeta_{t,1}\|_{\infty}
from Lemma
4
with that in Lemma
9
above. While in the former, this bound is on the order of
\mathcal{O}(1)
, it is on the order of
\mathcal{O}(T^{p})
in the latter. As a result, if one were to directly use the bound from Lemma
9
in the standard Azuma Hoeffding inequality (much like what we do in Lemma
5
), the resulting final bounds would be vacuous. This calls for a more intricate analysis. In this context, our next result provides a finer bound on
\|\zeta_{t,1}\|_{\infty}
; however, the price of this finer bound is that it now only holds with high probability.

Lemma 10
.

(Finer Probabilistic Bounds on Iterates for
Robust Async-RAQ
)

The following bounds hold with probability at least
1-2\delta_{1}T
for all
t\in[T]
:

\displaystyle\lvert\eta_{t,1}(s_{t},a_{t})\rvert

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}$}}{\raise \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{6 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{12 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{12 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{12 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{12 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{12 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{12 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{12 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{12 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{12 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{12 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{12 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{12 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{12 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{12 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{12 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{12 \mc{C} \tilde{\sigma}}{1-\gamma}}},

(96)

where
\mathcal{C}
is the universal constant that appears in (
6
).

Proof.

Let us start by revisiting the bounds on the reward proxy
\tilde{r}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})
established in Lemma
8
. In the proof of Lemma
8
, we established that for
t\leq\tilde{T}
,
|\tilde{r}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})|\leq 3\mathcal{C}\tilde{\sigma}

deterministically
. Furthermore, we also showed that for
t>\tilde{T}
, the following are true with probability at least
1-2\delta_{1}T
: (i)
\tilde{r}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})=\bar{r}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})
, and (ii)
|\bar{r}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})|\leq G_{t}
, where
G_{t}
is as in (
6
). Since
G_{t}\leq 3\mathcal{C}\tilde{\sigma},\forall t\geq\bar{T}
, we conclude that there exists an event of measure at least
1-2\delta_{1}T
, on which,
|\tilde{r}_{t}(s_{t},a_{t})|\leq 3\mathcal{C}\tilde{\sigma},\forall t\geq 0
. Restricted to this good event, one can now perform the exact same analysis as in the proof of Lemma
4
to establish the claim of this lemma.
∎

Based on the previous two results, we now have a martingale difference which exhibits a crude deterministic upper bound, and a finer bound that holds with a fixed high probability. We are in need of a refined version of the Azuma Hoeffding inequality that can exploit this structure. Thankfully,
[
33
, Theorem 7]
provides us with precisely the right tool. Our result in Theorem
5
is a slight modification of this theorem. For completeness, we include its proof below.

Proof of Theorem
5
.

The core idea behind the proof is to carefully construct a new martingale
\{Y_{0},Y_{1},\ldots,Y_{n}\}
that satisfies the following two properties simultaneously: (i) the martingale differences are “well-behaved" in the sense that
|Y_{i+1}-Y_{i}|=\mathcal{O}(c_{i}),\forall i\geq 0

deterministically
, and (ii)
|Y_{n}-X_{n}|
is “small" on a good event of sufficient measure. To achieve this, let us start by using
\mathcal{F}_{i}
to denote the event
\lvert X_{i+1}-X_{i}\rvert>c_{i}
. Next, set
Y_{0}=X_{0}
and let
p=\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{F}_{i}\lvert X_{i})
. Assuming
Y_{i}
has been already defined, we consider two cases:

(A)

If
p\geq r^{\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}}}
, terminate the martingale by setting
Y_{j}=Y_{i}
for all
j\in[i+1,n]
.

(B)

If
p<r^{\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}}}
, and the martingale has not been previously terminated, define:

\bar{X}_{i+1}=\left\{\linespread\begin{array}[]{@{}l@{}>{\quad}l@{}}X_{i}&\text{if }\mathcal{F}_{i},\\
X_{i+1}&\text{otherwise}.\end{array}\right.

We now have:

\mathbb{E}[\bar{X}_{i+1}\lvert X_{i}]=\mathbb{E}[X_{i+1}\lvert X_{i}]+\mathbb{E}[\bar{X}_{i+1}-X_{i+1}\lvert X_{i}]=X_{i}+A_{i},

(97)

where
A_{i}\triangleq\mathbb{E}[\bar{X}_{i+1}-X_{i+1}\lvert X_{i}]
. Then:

A_{i}=\mathbb{E}[\bar{X}_{i+1}-X_{i+1}\lvert X_{i},\mathcal{F}_{i}]\cdot\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{F}_{i}\lvert X_{i}).

Using the crude bound
|X_{i+1}-X_{i}|\leq b_{i}
and
p=\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{F}_{i}\lvert X_{i})<r^{\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}}}
, we obtain:

A_{i}\leq b_{i}\cdot r^{\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}}},

(98)

where we used the condition for Case B.
With this preparation, we define the sequence
\{Y_{i}\}
recursively as follows:

Y_{i+1}=Y_{i}+(\bar{X}_{i+1}-X_{i}-A_{i}).

Our immediate goal is to establish that
\{Y_{i+1}-Y_{i}\}
is a bounded martingale difference sequence. To establish the boundedness aspect, start by observing that

|\bar{X}_{i+1}-X_{i}|=|\bar{X}_{i+1}-X_{i}|\left(\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{F}_{i}}+\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{F}^{c}_{i}}\right)=|{X}_{i+1}-X_{i}|\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{F}^{c}_{i}}\leq c_{i},

where we used the definition of the event
\mathcal{F}_{i}
in the last step. Appealing to (
98
) and using
b_{i}\cdot r^{\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}}}\leq c_{i}
, we then obtain

\lvert Y_{i+1}-Y_{i}\rvert\leq c_{i}+b_{i}\cdot r^{\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}}}\leq 2c_{i}.

Next, using (
97
) and the definition of
Y_{i+1}
, observe that
\mathbb{E}[Y_{i+1}-Y_{i}\lvert Y_{i}]=0
. Thus,
\{Y_{n}\}_{n\geq 1}
is indeed a martingale with bounded martingale differences. To proceed, let
\mathcal{G}
be the “good event" where Case A never occurs, and
\mathcal{F}_{i}
never occurs. On this event, it follows from our construction that

Y_{n}=X_{n}-\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{i=0}^{n-1}A_{i}.

Therefore, we get the following deterministic bound on event
\mathcal{G}
:

|Y_{n}-X_{n}|=\left|\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{i=0}^{n-1}A_{i}\right|\leq r^{\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}}}\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{i=0}^{n-1}b_{i}.

(99)

Thus, on the good event, the above display provides control over the difference between our martingale of interest
\{X_{n}\}
, and the martingale we constructed
\{Y_{n}\}
. To gain control over the bad event
\mathcal{G}^{c}
, our next task is to get a bound on
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{G}^{c})
. To that end, we will require the following estimate:

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\left(\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{F}_{i}\lvert X_{i})>r^{\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{\mathbb{E}[

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{

0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{i=0}^{n-1}b_{i}\cdot r^{1/2}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(**)}{\leq}\mathbb{P}\left[\Bigg\{|X_{n}-Y_{n}|>\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{i=0}^{n-1}b_{i}\cdot r^{1/2}\Bigg\}\cup\Bigg\{|Y_{n}-Y_{0}|>\sqrt{\left(32\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{n}c_{i}^{2}\right)\log\left(\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2}{\delta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2}{\delta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2}{\delta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2}{\delta}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2}{\delta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2}{\delta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2}{\delta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2}{\delta}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2}{\delta}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2}{\delta}}}\right)}\Bigg\}\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(***)}{\leq}\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{G}^{\texttt{C}})+\mathbb{P}\left[|Y_{n}-Y_{0}|>\sqrt{\left(32\mathop{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\sum$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\sum$}}}}\displaylimits_{i=1}^{n}c_{i}^{2}\right)\log\left(\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2}{\delta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2}{\delta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2}{\delta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2}{\delta}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2}{\delta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2}{\delta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2}{\delta}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2}{\delta}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2}{\delta}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{2}{\delta}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{2}{\delta}}}\right)}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 2nr^{\mathchoice{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}}}{\mathord{\mathchoice{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\textstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}{\raise 0.49991pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}$}}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}}{\mathchoice{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{0}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{1}{1}{2}}{\genfrac{}{}{}{2}{1}{2}}}}+\delta.

In step
(*)
, we apply the triangle inequality, which states that
|X_{n}-X_{0}|\leq|X_{n}-Y_{n}|+|Y_{n}-Y_{0}|
, allowing us to bound the original probability by replacing
|X_{n}-X_{0}|
with
|X_{n}-Y_{n}|+|Y_{n}-Y_{0}|
. In step
(**)
, we use the union bound, which ensures that
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A}+\mathcal{B}>\mathcal{Q})\leq\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A}>\mathcal{Q}_{1})+\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{B}>\mathcal{Q}_{2})
, where
\mathcal{Q}_{1}+\mathcal{Q}_{2}=\mathcal{Q}
. Finally, in step
(*\hskip-1.42262pt*\hskip-1.42262pt*)
, we use the bound
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{G}^{\texttt{C}})\leq 2nr^{1/2}
for the first term, as
|X_{n}-Y_{n}|
is controlled by the good event
\mathcal{G}
, and the second term is bounded by
\delta
via an application of Azuma-Hoeffding (Lemma
3
) to the martingale
Y_{n}
. With this, our proof is complete.
∎

Armed with the previous result, we are now in a position to control the noise term in
Robust Async-RAQ
.

Proof.

using the probabilistic variant of the Azuma–Hoeffding inequality outlined in Theorem

(104)

To satisfy the condition
b_{i}\cdot r^{1/2}\leq c_{i}
that is required to apply Theorem
5
, it suffices to ensure:

(2\delta_{1}T)^{1/2}\cdot T^{p}\leq\tilde{\sigma}.

(105)

Since
\tilde{\sigma}\geq 1
, the above condition can be ensured by requiring

(2\delta_{1}T)^{1/2}\cdot T^{p}\leq 1,

(106)

which ensures the applicability of the refined concentration bound. Now, Eq. (
106
) imposes the following condition on the failure probability:
\delta_{1}\leq 1/(2T^{2p+1})
. Assuming that the requirement in Eq. (
106
) holds, then for a fixed
(s,a)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}
and

, Theorem
5
, when applied with the parameter choices in Eq. (
104
), implies that with probability at least
